- **rendering**: For the optical code, start at `shadeGlass()` in `shaders/glass_core.glsl`: it maps coordinates and displacement, then calls `sampleGlass`, `gradeInput`, `reflectGlass`, `gradeFace`, `lightGlass`, `gradePressed` and `hdrRim` in paint order. `glass.frag` declares the scalar packet; `glass_batch.frag` unpacks a member before entering the same routine. Dart packet assembly is in `_RenderGlass`. ([rehmatsg/liquid_prism](https://github.com/rehmatsg/liquid_prism/blob/HEAD/doc/agents/rendering.md))
- **rendering**: `sprites/relics.ts` maps all 129 actual catalog relics to their own native PNGs, using exact names so preview copies resolve without importing gameplay modules into the artwork layer. `relicIcon` accepts either a named relic or a generic rarity; collection cells, event tracks/store offers, tower Goals and enlarged popups pass the actual relic, while navigation and unknown future entries retain the ([sethrimer3/TowerProject](https://github.com/sethrimer3/TowerProject/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/rendering.md))
